# Timed recall on your own collection (SM121): result

**What answer time means.** The answer time is the whole time on the card, front and back together: from the moment the question is shown to the moment a grade button is pressed, capped at the preset's maximum answer seconds. The "Show answer" moment is not stored, so front-only and back-only times cannot be computed. Every "within S s" below means answered within S s, reading the back included.

Scoring only, CPU, on your own collection (the copy of 2026-10-14, reviews up to 2026-10-04; aggregate numbers only). Pre-registered in `design.md`. Reliable = ECE (20 equal-mass bins) below 0.01 and LogLoss below every baseline of the bar (recent history and card history, listed below) with the 95% CI below zero (2,000 day-block bootstraps, seed 0; card-block bootstraps as a sensitivity). Deltas are head minus baseline. LogLoss is compared only within the same outcome and threshold; the untimed grade events have their own table.

Rows: bench/full 173,384, bench/yomitan 153,435, mac/full 35,256, mac/yomitan 26,947, mac_date/full 33,345, mac_date/yomitan 25,036. `bench` = the benchmark test reviews with a recorded time (the 732-set row rule: same-day reviews included, first reviews excluded); `mac` = the Mac's slice (later-day reviews with a prior review and a positive time, latest 20% chronologically, from 2026-04); `mac_date` = the same filter from 28 April 2026 on. `yomitan` = the Yomitan decks (2 decks).

Baselines (which earlier reviews feed each one): (1) raw P(set), time ignored; (2) P(set) x the share of earlier answers of the set within S s and (3) sum_g P(g) x the share of earlier answers of grade g within S s, both over every earlier time-labelled review (positive time, not a first review, same-day included) with pseudo-count 10 at the pooled priors; (b) the running rate of the event over the same reviews; (b2) its rate over the previous 2,000 of them; (4) and (5) = (2) and (3) adjusted by the card's last three earlier positive-time answers (any day, any grade; (k + 3 x share) / (n + 3)); the Mac's four (window = the preceding 2,000 eligible reviews, i.e. later-day with a positive time, in the scope: the whole collection, or only the Yomitan decks for the Yomitan rows): R x recent, grade x recent, R x card, grade x card (card strength 3; 1 and 10 as a sensitivity outside the bar).

## Does it discriminate between reviews, or is it a big average?

Head vs the card-history baseline (card-adjusted recent shares / bin distributions). Spread = across reviews; AUC given Good = on reviews graded Good, P(t <= S | Good) ranks the fast ones from the slow ones (timing only, the grade is fixed); resolution (Murphy) is the part of the Brier score that is more than an average; R^2 of the predicted mean ln time on uncensored Good reviews; within-card = each review's predicted ln median minus that card's mean prediction, correlated with the same for the actual ln time (does it follow a card getting faster or slower). Day-block CIs on head minus card.

| combination | slice | SD / IQR of predicted median given Good, head vs card (s) | P(within 4 s given Good) p10-p90, head vs card | AUC given Good at 4 s head / card / recent (diff CI) | resolution GE within 4 s head / card (diff CI) | R^2 head / card / recent | within-card corr head / card (diff CI) |

Decile lift tables (observed median time and fast rate per decile of the predicted median, Good and all grades, head and card), every S, every slice, Brier, card-block CIs, reliability tables with counts, fixed 10-bin calibration: `sm121-result.json`. The reproduction control of the Mac's numbers is separate: `sm121-repro.md`.
